## Phase 520 — Databento security master PIT identity feasibility update (2026-11-20)

- Current official documentation advertises 21 years of PIT security-master history (2005-01-01 to present), 860,000+ securities and 215 venues; the feed guide says weekly updates and listing-level records. Reference API `security_master.get_range` supports `ts_effective` and `ts_record`, up to 2,000 symbols per request, and exposes listing/security/issuer IDs, listing/delisting dates, CIK, CUSIP/US code, ISIN and FIGI.
- The exact 335 Phase 21C research snapshot fits one documented request, but its exact coverage has not been tested. The snapshot is not a historical active-universe membership table; date-appropriate listed/active status remains mandatory.
- Strong candidate for the effective-dated identity bridge for FINRA T1, SEC FTD and SEC ownership data. `ts_record` is only a candidate record-known clock: public docs do not define it as exact first public availability or quantify upstream lag; weekly update frequency must be measured and confirmed.
- Identity limits: documented continuity across name/ticker changes, mergers and demergers; no continuity for spin-offs or securities permanently delisted without relisting. Security master does not provide terminal delisting returns. Retain the independent CRSP/price-source gate.
- Pricing is internally inconsistent on the product page (Standard: $0.50/additional symbol/month; FAQ Starter: $1.75); obtain a written quote. New-user $125 credits are advertised. Internal use is permitted; external redistribution is prohibited. Do not put raw licensed rows in public artifacts.
- No account, trial, purchase, API key, API request or raw download. Next gate is an authorized one-shot 335-symbol pilot with aggregate-only output after `DATABENTO_API_KEY` and terms are confirmed. No alpha/OOS/returns test; F80 baseline remains frozen.
- Report: [Phase 520](research/PHASE520_DATABENTO_SECURITY_MASTER_PIT_IDENTITY_FEASIBILITY_2026-11-20.md) · [summary](research/PHASE520_DATABENTO_SECURITY_MASTER_PIT_IDENTITY_FEASIBILITY_SUMMARY_2026-11-20.json).
- Official sources: https://databento.com/security-master · https://databento.com/docs/api-reference-data/security-master?lang=python · https://databento.com/docs/datasets/security-master · https://indexes.morningstar.com/research-data-products/crsp-us-stock-databases.
 
### Phase 520 vendor update — Databento Reference API migration (2026-11-20)

- Databento's 2026-11-10 announcement says production `listing_status` currently repeats the latest status across historical rows; it is not safe for historical as-of status before the 2026-12-10 cutover. The preview gateway `hist-preview.databento.com` has corrected period-specific status now. Pilot on preview, version the gateway, and compare production after Dec 10.
- `nasdaq_symbol` formatting for some NYSE class/suffix securities changes; use stable identifiers/CUSIP/FIGI for joins. Product-page coverage counts also differ (860,000+ headline versus 310,000+ listed/delisted body count); clarify with vendor.
- Source: https://databento.com/blog/reference-api-changes-2026-11.

Its dataset page lists 126 columns and session-day `available_date`; the homepage example says 127 fields and next-day availability. Treat the PIT formula and field count as unresolved until vendor confirmation; no same-day-close execution for full-session aggregates.

Short-interest `available_date` formula and ticker-only identity need validation. Obscura Form 4 rows may be used only as a cross-check after multiple-owner and amendment reconciliation.

No account, subscription, API key, query, download or vendor contact. Keep any licensed raw/derived data and artifacts private; do not put them in this public repository without redistribution rights.

## Phase 555 — SEC 13D/G rule-clock and hypothesis priority (2026-11-22)

- Current official SEC Regulation 13D-G interpretations were last updated 2026-11-17. Initial Schedule 13D is due five business days after the acquisition trade date; material 13D amendments are due within two business days. Schedule 13G deadlines vary by filer category and are generally slower for quarterly material-change updates. SEC guidance updated 2026-09-02 also makes clear that 13G does not automatically mean no engagement or no influence.
- Re-rank a rebuilt Schedule 13D event panel ahead of broad 13F/N-PORT ownership-change work. Keep 13G separate as slower holder-state/context data; do not pool the two forms or treat 13G as uniformly passive.
- The historical 20,266-row Phase 28D/35 panel remains quarantined: Phase 484 confirmed filer-vs-subject issuer misattribution; Phase 485's separate SEC-HEADER SUBJECT COMPANY / FILED BY parser is not yet applied to the full panel. ## Phase 564 — SEC FTD release cadence, balance semantics and PIT gate (2026-11-22)

- Official SEC Fails-to-Deliver Data documentation now explicitly confirms the post-July-2009 release pattern: first-half files at month-end; second-half files about the 15th of the following month. The SEC also explicitly does **not** guarantee posting by a particular date or data accuracy. Pre-July-2009 files are monthly settlement-date files archived quarterly.
- FTD is an aggregate NSCC CNS **outstanding net balance** across all members, not new daily fails, short-sale volume, short interest, or participant identity. It includes carryover + new fails − settled fails; the age/source of individual fails cannot be inferred. Fails may arise from long or short sales. Pre-2008-09-16 reporting omits balances below 10,000 shares; post-boundary zero balances have no row.
- PIT policy: settlement date is not the knowledge date. The documented cadence is approximate, not strict PIT. Use prospective append-only first-seen timestamps; historical lag variants remain sensitivity-only. Post-2008 absent rows may be zero only within a complete validated file and a valid effective-dated CUSIP mapping; otherwise unknown. Pre-2008 absence is threshold-censored.
- Existing fixed-335 FTD coverage diagnostics (253/335 in 2018H1; 316/335 in 2026H2) remain non-historical-universe diagnostics, not date-appropriate coverage.
- Latest known run audit: [45056358765](https://github.com/jafelix81/NuevaEstrategia/actions/runs/45056358765) was a successful offline-only PR check (16 tests, no artifact); latest known natural Form 144 capture remains [45052961790](https://github.com/jafelix81/NuevaEstrategia/actions/runs/45052961790), both jobs successful, artifact [14765739720](https://github.com/jafelix81/NuevaEstrategia/actions/runs/45052961790/artifacts/14765739720). This connector does not expose a repository-wide Actions run-list endpoint, so no newer full run census or active-run claim is made.
